# 03 — Full Target Folder & File Structure (all-in layout)

This is the **complete** tree for the maximal version being evaluated: **every module in its own
folder** with its own `src/` and `tests/`, and **every layer as its own project**. It is generated by
mapping **every real file** in the current `src/` and `tests/` to its new home and injecting the new
scaffolding (`.csproj`, `Directory.*.props`, design-time factories, `.slnf` filters). **No `...` — every
file is listed.**

> This is the *all-in* layout so you can see it in full — including **per-module unit AND integration
> test projects**. The honest recommendation is **not** to go all the way to this — see
> [02-decision-b-layers-as-projects](02-decision-b-layers-as-projects.md),
> [05-testing-strategy](05-testing-strategy.md) and
> [07-migration-plan-and-verdict](07-migration-plan-and-verdict.md). Main deltas of the *recommended*
> pragmatic layout vs. this tree: (a) endpoints stay in `<M>.Application` (no separate `<M>.Api`
> project — a dedicated presentation project shreds the vertical slices); (b) `Core` and `Mailer` are
> **not** layer-split; (c) **integration tests are NOT actually split per module** — the harness is
> whole-app, so in practice keep one shared integration project (shown per-module here only to render
> the maximal layout). See [05](05-testing-strategy.md) for the ~4× Testcontainers reason.

- Total files placed: **3841** across **36 projects**.
- Mapping rules (deterministic, applied to every file):
  - `src/Modules/<M>/<M>/Domain/**` → `modules/<M>/src/<M>.Domain/**`
  - `src/Modules/<M>/<M>/Application/**` (except endpoints) → `modules/<M>/src/<M>.Application/**`
  - `src/Modules/<M>/<M>/Application/**/V1/*EndpointV1.cs` and `*MetaField.cs` → `modules/<M>/src/<M>.Api/**` (presentation)
  - `src/Modules/<M>/<M>/Infrastructure/**` (incl. `Persistence`, `Migrations`, `Repositories`, `Services`) → `modules/<M>/src/<M>.Infrastructure/**`
  - `src/Modules/<M>/<M>/<M>Module.cs` → `modules/<M>/src/<M>.Infrastructure/<M>Module.cs`
  - `src/Modules/<M>/<M>.Contracts/**` → `modules/<M>/src/<M>.Contracts/**`
  - `src/Shared/Shared/Domain/**` → `shared/src/Shared.Kernel/**`
  - `src/Shared/Shared/Application/**` → `shared/src/Shared.Application/**`
  - `src/Shared/Shared/Infrastructure/**` → `shared/src/Shared.Infrastructure/**`
  - `src/Shared/Shared.Contracts/**` → `shared/src/Shared.Contracts/**`
  - `src/BuildingBlocks/**` → `shared/src/BuildingBlocks/**`
  - `src/Api/**` → `host/Api/**`
  - **Unit tests** `tests/Unit/Modules/<M>/**` → `modules/<M>/tests/<M>.Unit.Tests/**`
  - **Integration tests** `tests/Integration/Modules/<M>/**` → `modules/<M>/tests/<M>.Integration.Tests/**`
  - **Shared integration harness** `tests/Integration/Common/**` (WebApplicationFactory, Postgres/Testcontainers fixtures, seeders, stubs) → `shared/tests/Shared.TestKit/Integration/**`
  - **Cross-module workflow tests** `tests/Integration/Workflows/**` (no single-module owner) → `host/tests/EndToEnd.Tests/Workflows/**`
  - `tests/Integration/Shared/**`, `tests/Integration/coverage/**` → `shared/tests/Shared.Integration.Tests/**`
  - Shared test data `tests/Fixtures/**`, `tests/Unit/Common/**` → `shared/tests/Shared.TestKit/**`
  - `tests/Unit/Shared/**`, `tests/Unit/BuildingBlocks/**` → `shared/tests/Shared.Unit.Tests/**`
- Notes: `Core` has **no** `Core.Api` project (library module, 0 HTTP endpoints). `Content` has **no**
  `Content.Contracts` (not consumed by other modules). `Core.Contracts`, `Shared.Kernel`, and the split
  `Shared.*` projects are **new** (created during the move). Per-module `<M>.Integration.Tests` projects
  are shown for completeness of the all-in layout; each would still boot the whole-app harness from
  `Shared.TestKit`, which is why the study recommends **one** shared integration project instead —
  [05-testing-strategy](05-testing-strategy.md).
